Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The coding variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 944 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 569 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to coding structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 944/569 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 212.4 (Invariance Principle)
